Possible duplicate people

Cross-cycle, distinct resolved person profiles. Names require shared office or city within 12 years. Candidate generation only; no identity merges. Existing aliases already collapse approved identities.

12446 resolved people checked; 658 candidate pairs, 106 with stronger internal evidence, 656 absent from the existing card-review ledger. No production data changed.

Shared offices, party labels, and compatible names do not prove identity. Generic offices and geography can still conceal namesakes. Nickname coverage is bounded; arbitrary name changes and duplicates already collapsed into one ID are outside this audit.

Evidence below comes from imported source records; this pass made no network requests. “Stronger” means higher priority for source review, not confirmed identity. Prior dispositions are retained and must be honored.
